- WINDOW-PING-READY fixed the window-ping e2e flake at its cause: pings were stamped with the main-thread input tick but expired against the Worker's sim tick, so on a slow runner every ping was born expired; pings now carry the sim tick, and the e2e waits on a new `window.__keyrunInputReady` flag and prints tick diagnostics on failure. (merged e2f9c81 as #205)

- 2026-10-20 CRASH-RECOVERY-LOG (docs/crash-recovery-log): the machine crash lost nothing and development kept going; this entry is the live demo of the Aesop kernel. Matt, verbatim: "i want to log the recovery and continued progress ... that is the demo of the kernel, we did not lose anything and identified 100 issues lmfao". Crash: Windows Kernel-Power event 41 hard freeze at 2026-10-20 11:38:25 CDT (checked: Get-WinEvent System log). Earlier freeze 2026-10-17 13:44:56 CDT (checked, same log), cause unknown. Cause as reported: many parallel lanes launching the game (Godot/keyrun.exe) during the rewrite dispatch, plus lanes running the whole hook test suite at once (a node process storm). Nothing lost: recovery reported that work was recovered from GitHub origin and wip backup branches and that git fsck came back clean (fsck not re-run here; the other repos were not checked by this entry). Safeguards: keyrun #301 single-instance Godot launch lock, MERGED as 69a0e1e2e4ad45609170685f68cd96b276e87bd3 (checked: gh pr view 301); lanes are no longer allowed to launch the game; only the orchestrator captures frames, one at a time. Issues found: fuzz audits of the brain hooks found about 195 defects (A 39, B 41, C 65, E 37, F 13), most of them hooks that failed open; they are being fixed with before/after proof. Keyrun progress since the crash (gh pr view, checked 2026-10-20). MERGED: #297 "fix(sim): the Sundered Twins die with the Hollow Warden"; #301 "fix(tools): single-instance Godot launch lock, wall-clock cap, tree kill". OPEN: #302 "feat(sim): stops, caster ops and the slam circle (forces-route M2 + increment 5, BIBLE s3)"; #303 "docs(state): record launch lock, orchestrator-only captures, D15 flow"; #304 "rewrite(R3): Mythic+ timer tiers and Fortified/Tyrannical affixes (M1, M5)"; #305 "feat(playtest): agent playtesters that can fail, learn and push keys"; #306 "docs(state): D30 aggro direction - body pulls stay, pull warning, no threat meter, KK tank-mark swap"; #307 "feat(sim): holder lock - engaged enemies stay on their holder until a taunt (D30)"; #308 "feat(sim,bots): every group prespawns idle and engages on its aggro radius (PRESPAWN-AGGRO, KF3)"; #309 "fix(bots): a bot party leads itself into the Choir instead of idling at the Entrance (KF2)"; #310 "fix(view): an instant swing is drawn facing whom it struck (KF1-FACING)"; #311 "docs: D31 skill toolkit direction (Outlaw Rogue toolkit on five buttons)". API usage-limit refusals expected later today; logged in a follow-up entry.

## D-HEALER-DOWNTIME (dungeon loop -> WoW rule)
Evidence: #362 fix lane afaf604623448b480 measured that with Warden armor 3500 bp the Chanter bot's Cadence casts fall 6 -> 1 and hymn-field combos 5 -> 1 on the Choir, because Healer.cs:122-127 (port of healer.ts) acts in combat only when an ally is under 70%. Run C (RunCCarelessPairTests.cs:117) then runs out of ticks. Armor sweep: any armor >= 1500 bp breaks run C, so lowering armor would be fitted green.
Ruling (WoW Mythic+: a healer with nobody to heal keeps contributing, it never idles): in combat with no ally under the heal threshold, the healer bot presses its non-heal buttons (setups/damage) on the party's target. Cadence and Hymn stay heals. Change both game/bots/Keyrun.Bots/Roles/Healer.cs and bots/src/roles/healer.ts (parity), regenerate the oracle with its writer. Bible test served: "Depth and difficulty" (five buttons with real decisions) and s4 synergy as a timing game.
Also ruled: HudSceneTest.ps1 solo wipe runs A, B, E get 900 ticks instead of 600 because a Warden with 3500 bp armor dies at tick 505 (run_end 606) by measurement; this is a harness budget for an intended change, not an assertion weakened to hide a defect. No other threshold moves.

## D-CADENCE-UNMEASURED (#370)
Evidence: lane ac2bceed7702f606f showed chanter.cadence IS cast on both trees on the same ticks (t305/t308/t263); its options change heal counts (81/83/101/80) but never reach clear tick, deaths or Combos, on main too, because the bot only casts Cadence as a heal (HealPick.cs:69) so the Attuned window lands on an ally while the Anthem/Flashfire payoffs (combos.json:31,:37) hit enemies. On main cadence counted as measured only through knock-on noise. Dominance, identical-siblings and identical-runs checks pass on the merged tree.
Ruling: add chanter.cadence to UnmeasuredForks on #370 with the TRUE reason ("cast only as a heal; Attuned never closes into a Combo in bot play"), and open a finding for the real gap: Cadence's setup role (BIBLE s4/s5) is dead in bot play on main. That gap is a bot/content feature, dispatched after the pile.

Chain: tick 2048 the Pair engages the careless Cinder (seat 3, 250 HP) and all five Pair enemies lock onto it; ScriptedWarden never taunts (no taunt in its rotation) so under the lock it can never take the pack back; tick 2108 four pair.meleeSwing (70 each, no cast time, no cast bar) land on the same tick and seat 3 goes from full to dead in one tick; then all four spreadMarks land on the Warden, who dies at 2549; healer 2696; Depleted 2797. Once threat is initialized, it cant be taken except for abilities"): (1) who holds after a non-tank body pull is the puller, as in WoW (mobs aggro whoever pulled) and as #306 says; the HolderLockTests body-pull assertion stays, experiment row 8 is rejected. (2) Recovery is the tank's job, as in WoW: ScriptedWarden taunts the nearest enemy held by another living player when Taunt is ready and in range (the bot tank's PickLooseEnemy rule), red-first test. (3) Four enemies auto-attacking on the same tick for a lethal burst with no cast bar is a sim artifact, not WoW, where auto-attacks desync by arrival and swing timer: enemy melee swings get a per-enemy phase (first swing offset by arrival or index) so auto-attacks alone cannot take a seat from full to dead between two ticks; failing test first; meleeSwing damage, seat HP, the Expected and the scenario numbers never change. (4) WoW Taunt is an 8 s cooldown; warden.taunt cooldownTicks (160) may be retuned toward 8 s at the sim's tick rate only with a measured before/after lever in the commit body, measured with a script file or the Edit tool, never a sed carrying a `$`. (5) Taunt stays single-target (WoW Taunt is single-target; an AoE taunt is a separate ability decision, not taken). (6) If (2)+(3)+(4) still do not reach Timed, the lane reports the per-tick evidence and stops; no fit.

- 2026-10-20 #249 CLOSED fix/content-text-matches-data: CONTENT-TEXT rewrote twelve Facet and talent texts to say what their patches do and added a Content test that fails on any text claim the patch data does not back.
- 2026-10-20 #251 MERGED feat/view-ping-speaks-party-language: PING-FEEDBACK: pressing G now shows a 2 s party-language word (kick, Window!, stack here, focus, here) over the pinged target or spot, chosen from the same state the bots' ping reactions read; gate PingWordsTests.cs.
- 2026-10-20 #252 CLOSED fix/view-damage-numbers-clear-nameplates: DMG-NUMBERS moved the human's floating damage numbers and MISS text beside the target's drawn nameplate and out of every cast bar, plate and HUD block, so neither the number nor the plate and cast label are hidden.
- 2026-10-20 #253 MERGED fix/view-run-timer-stops-at-clear: 2026-10-20 RUN-TIMER-STOP (fix/view-run-timer-stops-at-clear): verified real (the HUD timer read the live run clock, which StepRun keeps advancing after run_end); fixed by reading the sim's run-end tick through RunClock.ShownTicks, with red-then-green RunClockTests and a naive-wrong (return ResultTick) mutant caught.
- 2026-10-20 #254 MERGED test/talent-patch-parity-cs-ts: TALENT-PARITY adds a C#/TS parity test for the talent patch path over all 38 shipped options (TS snapshot plus Keyrun.Content.Tests comparison), with the four Chanter verse-variant deviations and six TS-refused second-Payoff facets as named known mismatches.
- 2026-10-20 #255 MERGED fix/run-uses-picked-talents: 2026-10-20 TALENT-PICKS (fix/run-uses-picked-talents): picked talents now reach the queued run through QueueRequest.Loadouts into RunOptions.Loadouts (5 new xUnit cases red on base by compile and red again with the loadout dropped from ToRunOptions or from Queue; ScreenSceneTest red on base and red with the Screen wiring removed), and the talent panel's applied= count and caption are now true.
- 2026-10-20 #256 MERGED fix/bots-kick-choir-casts: BOT-CHOIR-KICK: ChoirKickTests refutes KF2's kick half, the bots kick all 5 Choral Mendings in pull 1 with the human idle, and guards the kick ledger from here on.
- 2026-10-20 #257 MERGED fix/view-circle-telegraph-visible: TELEGRAPH-CIRCLE made circle floor telegraphs visible (the boss slam, twin slam, seed slam and Pair spread/stack circles drew zero-area fans before) via TelegraphFan.Span1024, red then green in TelegraphFanTests and proven in a windowed capture of the boss slam disc.
- 2026-10-20 #258 MERGED fix/hud-charge-cooldown-next-charge: 2026-10-20 CHARGE-COOLDOWN (fix/hud-charge-cooldown-next-charge): the ability bar's cooldown number for a two-charge ability is the wait until the next charge, so Charge spent twice reads 9.5 s instead of 19.5 s; ChargeReadoutTests was red with the HUD formula ("Expected: 190 Actual: 390") and green with the fix, and a two-press replay printed "charges=0/2 shown=19.5 tick=31" before and "shown=9.5 tick=31" after, with the charge back at tick 220.
- 2026-10-20 #259 MERGED fix/content-facet-promises-kept: FACET-PROMISES gave Overflow Valve a real partial dump (40 Heat kept) and Cheap Dissonance a real upside (cooldown 60 to 40), proven by casting the patched abilities through the real loadout and sim cast path.
- 2026-10-20 #260 MERGED fix/content-no-enemy-line-telegraph: NO-ENEMY-LINE-TELEGRAPH added a content test that rejects enemy line telegraphs until TelegraphView can draw them, so an invisible line attack cannot ship (red with seed.slam set to line, green on main).
- 2026-10-20 #261 MERGED fix/hud-stack-drawn-once: HUD-STACK-ONCE draws "STACK: group up" once, in the callout banner, and marks the stack spot with the word STACK alone.
- 2026-10-20 #262 MERGED fix/hud-what-got-you-one-row-per-name: WHAT-GOT-YOU: the run-end "What got you" list merges actors sharing a display name into one row (CauseRows.MergeByName), so run C reads "Choir Acolyte 1715 in 32 hits" once instead of 1183/22 and 532/10; the healthDamage-vs-label suspicion was refuted.
- 2026-10-20 #263 MERGED fix/combat-text-reads-plate-rects: PLATE-RECTS made CombatText read LabelView's drawn plate rects instead of a copy that never folded same-name crowds, so damage numbers no longer land on a stacked "x2" plate (27 of 155 numbers did on the DMG-NUMBERS branch, 0 of 156 after).
- 2026-10-20 #264 MERGED fix/content-surge-text-no-false-synergy: SURGE-TEXT removed four Facet promises of tag synergies no game system reads, keeping only the charge and resource gains the patches deliver, with the text-vs-data walk now rejecting synergy claims and unbacked resource gains.
- 2026-10-20 #265 MERGED docs/code-decision-ids-match-state: DECISION-IDS re-checked every decision id cited in game/ code comments against STATE.md and design/BIBLE.md; two wrong D10 citations on the Space dash were dropped, all other citations matched.
- 2026-10-20 #266 MERGED fix/sim-facets-reach-the-run: 2026-10-20 FACET-APPLIED (fix/sim-facets-reach-the-run): equipped Facets now reach the queued run through GearFacets and Screen's run loadout (ScreenSceneTest red on base with no queued Facet and red again with the Screen facet layer disabled; 9 GearFacetsTests red with the shape and append merge removed), and a --requeue headless run shows Bulwark playing the mobileWard self-aura shape.
- 2026-10-20 #267 MERGED feat/sim-ascension-after-tier-cap: ASCENSION-SIM adds the pure Ascension.IsAscended query over Progression (every gear slot at Tier 10, no reset, no power) with three red-then-green tests; visibility, marks and any HUD surface stay unbuilt.
- 2026-10-20 #269 MERGED feat/hud-boss-break-bar: BREAK-BAR made the Sundered Twins' shared Break bar visible on the Hollow Warden's unit frame, filling as interrupts and controls drain the sim bar, flashing full on the stagger and resetting when the sim refills it.
- 2026-10-20 #270 MERGED fix/run-end-par-label-is-computed: 2026-10-20 WITHIN-PAR (fix/run-end-par-label-is-computed): verified real (Timer.FinishRun yields Timed whenever Slack is not depleted, so an over-par clean clear was titled "within par"); first half of the fix adds the pure RunEndTitle rule with red-then-green tests and a boundary (>= par) mutant caught.
- 2026-10-20 #271 MERGED fix/rewards-carry-content-facet-ids: REWARD-FACETS refuted the reward-facets finding and shipped RewardDropFacetsTests as a regression guard that passes on main and fails 3/3 when the roll uses the placeholder pools.
- 2026-10-20 #272 MERGED fix/warden-warcry-and-mobile-bulwark: WARDEN-WINDOWS rewrote the Warcry, Mobile Ward and Surge talent texts to match what the sim does with each patch (Warcry Sunders two in total, Mobile Ward braces only the Warden and still plants its floor patch, Surge adds a cooldown rather than costing one), with WardenTalentTextTests driving each patched ability through the cast pipeline against its shipped sentence.
- 2026-10-20 #273 MERGED fix/hud-pull-header-player-facing: HUD-PULL-HEADER stopped the HUD pull header from showing the content teaches note and raw boss mechanic ids; the header is now "Pull N/T  <name>" built by the new Godot-free PullHeader model, with a Content test over every shipped pull.
- 2026-10-20 #274 MERGED fix/cinder-overkill-text-and-warcry-quote: CINDER-WORDING rewrote the Cinder Overkill talent text to "Longer cooldown; hits harder." (Detonate already had a cooldown that Overkill lengthens, and the hit grows about 28 percent), with CinderTalentTextTests driving base and patched Detonate through the cast pipeline against the shipped sentence, and refreshed the stale Warcry quote in WarcrySunderedTests; oracle frozenSourceHash 756e0275 -> 865569cc and content-pull contentHash 22955250 -> 89fbfd0b re-recorded for the text change only.
- 2026-10-20 #275 MERGED ci/tests-cancel-superseded-pr-runs: CI-CONCURRENCY-TESTS adds a top-level concurrency block to tests.yml so superseded PR head runs are cancelled instead of queuing behind the runner pool (66 of 122 PR runs in a 37-minute window were superseded while still running, about 308 runner-minutes).
- 2026-10-20 #276 MERGED fix/hud-boss-cast-row-collapse: BOSS-CAST-ROW: the boss unit frame collapses its empty cast row while the boss is idle, so the Break bar sits directly under health, and the row reopens with the spell name for each live cast (BossCastRowSceneTest red 6 then PASS).
- 2026-10-20 #277 MERGED docs/content-claude-md-current: content/CLAUDE.md's ScriptStep and Break bar bullets were stale against sim/encounter/scripts.ts, sim/encounter/index.ts, content/data/enemies/pair.json and seed.json; rewritten to match the code.
- 2026-10-20 #278 MERGED docs/fresh-worktree-build: docs/fresh-worktree-build adds the fresh-worktree build-solutions step to README.md's Run section, because every new lane worktree failed its first build until someone ran build-solutions by hand and no doc said so.
- 2026-10-20 #279 MERGED fix/hud-run-end-panel-opaque: 2026-10-20 RUNEND-OPAQUE (fix/hud-run-end-panel-opaque): verified real (capture frames 09/10 on bc65a05f showed the "Cinder" plate through the "Run cleared -- within par" panel; the fill was alpha 0.94); fixed by making the run-end panel fill opaque, with a red-then-green headless scene test reading the panel's own fill proof line.
- 2026-10-20 #281 MERGED fix/hud-resource-bar-class-label: RESOURCE-LABEL replaces the hardcoded "Momentum" resource-bar label with ResourceLabel.For over the local player's class resource, so a Cinder or Chanter human reads Heat or Verse once human class selection ships (10 new Content tests; a fallback-to-Momentum mutant turns 5 red).
- 2026-10-20 #282 MERGED fix/sim-tide-drifts-floor-windows: TIDE-DRIFT makes Tide move floor Windows across the arena as the Clause text and bible promise, instead of drifting enemy telegraphs alone.
- 2026-10-20 #283 MERGED fix/cinder-quench-refund-on-kick: 2026-10-20 QUENCH-REFUND (fix/cinder-quench-refund-on-kick): Quench now refunds its 20 Heat only when the interrupt lands, per bible s5, in both the C# sim and the frozen TS twin, with the three bot oracle traces, the boss golden and their recorded hashes re-recorded for the whiffed bot kicks (red on origin/main 70 vs 50 Heat on a whiff, red again with the guard removed).
- 2026-10-20 #284 MERGED fix/boss-readability: 2026-10-20 BOSS-READABILITY (fix/boss-readability): verified real from capture frame 07 on main e45f14c (the boss rendered near-black, its pose unreadable); fixed by lifting the boss tint to 0xB4C0DC under a new 500-permille tint-brightness floor, red-then-green in ActorBindingTests, after-fix boss frames 07 and 08 captured on the branch; Twin plate stacking BLOCKED-OVERLAP (HostRunSetup.cs #233, LabelView.cs #263) and the missing Twin (A) frame REFUTED as by design.
- 2026-10-20 #285 MERGED feat/nameplates-mouseover: MOUSEOVER-KICK switches the human to the WoW input model (free cursor, hold right mouse to look) and sends the Q kick at the enemy under the cursor when there is one, else the target, through the unchanged cast/interrupt path (bible s3 slice b; nameplates with cast bars wait for #263).
- 2026-10-20 #286 MERGED design/bible-forces-route: BIBLE-FORCES-ROUTE: bible section 9 now specifies the forces + route layer Matt chose on 2026-10-20 (branching packs, a 100% forces bar before the Hollow Door, kickable caster packs, two hazard rooms), the research-audit gaps and Matt's 2026-10-20 decisions (talents, Clause, rewards, vault, prespawn with aggro distance) are folded into the bible, and FORCES-ROUTE-PLAN.md breaks the dungeon work into 13 build increments, PRESPAWN-AGGRO first.
- 2026-10-20 #287 MERGED fix/cast-facing: 2026-10-20 CAST-FACING (fix/cast-facing): verified real (ActorView drew the sim yaw, which only move commands set, so casters faced their last walk direction; the k2-s11 oracle trace has Cinders 177 degrees off their flare target), fixed with a render-only CastFacing.RenderYaw wired into ActorView, red-then-green CastFacingTests plus naive and destructive mutants, no trace or golden moved.
- 2026-10-20 #288 MERGED feat/route-content-schema: ROUTE-CONTENT-SCHEMA lands forces.json, packs.json and hazards.json with typed models and a fail-closed route validator (teaching pulls 600 of a 1000 bar; a section 9.2 fixture loads at West 1220 and East 1180).
- 2026-10-20 #289 MERGED fix/seed-pull-teaches: SEED-PULL-TEACHES room-entry pulls run their scripts from their own pull_start, so the Seed's first slam is telegraphed inside the fight instead of starting 8 ticks after it spawned (KF3).
- 2026-10-20 #290 MERGED fix/content-probes-scan-both-roots: CONTENT-PROBES-BOTH-ROOTS adds the ContentRoots test probe (both content roots) and moves EncounterTestKit and CastFacingTests onto it, unblocking PACK-CONTENT's game/content/data/enemies folder.
- 2026-10-20 #291 MERGED feat/fork-dominance-check: FORK-PARITY adds the bible s6 dominance check over all 15 forks (38 options x 3 bot runs); on main it finds 5 dominated options (guardBreak:surge, flare:efficient (dominated by Wildfire since #296), detonate:cheapShot, cadence:swift, hymn:staccato) and 4 forks the bots never cast (strike, emberBolt, refrain, dissonance), all listed as known failures pending a retune.
- 2026-10-20 #292 MERGED fix/target-frame-after-death: TARGET-FRAME-AFTER-DEATH made the cleared-run objective count the enemies still alive at run_end, so it no longer says "No enemies remain" while the target frame shows a living Sundered Twin.
- 2026-10-20 #294 MERGED fix/combat-anim-reads: 2026-10-20 COMBAT-ANIM-READS (fix/combat-anim-reads): verified real from Movie Maker frames (the swing clip started on the damage tick, so a skeleton's punch landed 8 to 10 ticks after its number, and a Warden mid-swing showed nothing when hit), fixed in the view with a measured contact-frame seek, a 3-tick hit flash and a kick flinch, red-then-green AnimTimingTests with three production mutants, the 260-tick autorun run log byte-identical before and after.
- 2026-10-20 #296 MERGED fix/wildfire-never-ends: 2026-10-20 WILDFIRE-NEVER-ENDS (fix/wildfire-never-ends): verified real (with cinder.flare:wildfire the three bot oracle runs never ended by tick 24000 because Flare's caster-centred 1.5 m burst hit nothing from range); fixed in Keyrun.Sim Targeting so a hostile aoe circle bursts at the target, red-then-green with mutants; follow-up: Wildfire now dominates Efficient in the FORK-PARITY measures (k2 1533/30 vs 1664/26, k10 1865/37 vs 1906/35), and the retune (Wildfire damage 80 measured non-dominated both ways) waits on content/data/classes/cinder.json held by #274.
- 2026-10-20 #297 MERGED fix/twins-die-with-boss: 2026-10-20 TWINS-DIE-WITH-BOSS (fix/twins-die-with-boss): verified real (Twin A alive on 30 HP at run_end, seed 1 key 2); fixed in BossRules.AfterCombat so standing Twins die on the boss's death tick with a death event, red-then-green TwinsDieWithBossTests plus implementation-removed and wrong-tick mutants, no trace moved (Matt 2026-10-20 decision).
- 2026-10-20 #299 MERGED fix/camera-framing: CAMERA-FRAMING: the default camera frames the Warden like WoW (5 m boom, -0.30 rad, 59 degree fov, wheel zoom 2.5..12 m) and draws him 279 permille of the frame tall instead of 92..144, sim log byte-identical.
- 2026-10-20 #300 MERGED feat/fast-loop: FAST-LOOP adds tools/fastcheck.ps1 (diff-mapped build plus tests, fail closed) and a BannedApiAnalyzers purity list on Keyrun.Sim and Keyrun.Content, so the per-step lane check is seconds long and an impure sim call fails at compile time; every test and CI step is unchanged.
- 2026-10-20 #304 MERGED rewrite/r3-timer-affixes-main: R3 lands Mythic+ timer tiers (timed is par plus Slack left, +1/+2/+3 key jumps, an untimed key keeps its level) and current-WoW Fortified/Tyrannical affixes (one rotating from key 5, both from key 10) with the key-set Clause layered on top.
- 2026-10-20 #309 MERGED fix/bots-engage-choir: BOTS-ENGAGE-CHOIR: WHY an all-bot party (no human leading) never left the Entrance because the leader seat's tank role has nothing to do while no enemy lives and every follower waits on the leader, so the Choir never spawned and never taught the kick; WHAT: Follow.LeadCommand walks a bot leader seat along the route and holds it in the next pull room until the party is in, BotPartyEngagesChoirTests red on main, green with the fix.
- 2026-10-20 #310 MERGED fix/kf1-combat-anim: 2026-10-20 KF1-FACING (fix/kf1-combat-anim): instant swings now draw facing their victim until the swinger moves, the victim dies or a cone/line/self ability resolves (render only, the sim Yaw and every oracle trace untouched), with red-then-green SwingFacingTests in CI against an implementation-removed mutant; stacks with #294 (swing timing, hit flash, kick flinch) and fix/enemy-engage (sim chaser facing).
- 2026-10-20 #313 MERGED fix/slam-telegraphs: 2026-10-20 SLAM-TELEGRAPH (fix/slam-telegraphs): red SlamTelegraphTests first (telegraph gone on every tick of the slam cast), then the spansCast opt-in; oracle frozenSourceHash, the k2/k10 setups (spansCast copied by the TS) and content-pull contentHash re-recorded, no trace hash moved.
- 2026-10-20 #314 MERGED feat/five-gear-slots: FIVE-GEAR-SLOTS: gear.json gives all five BIBLE s7 slots a tier-1 base with damageDoneBp/damageTakenBp per tier, and the summed five-slot stats reach the human's hits through RunOptions.Gear and Mitigation.DealDamage.
- 2026-10-20 #315 MERGED docs/gear-depth: GEAR-DEPTH recorded D32 (context-dependent "best": function Facets, affix-correct loadouts, pair Facets, armory swap per key, Ascension marks as the post-cap chase) with a sourced brief (research/13-gear-depth.md) and a gap table of gear on main; nothing implemented; BIBLE conflicts listed in the PR for Matt.
- 2026-10-20 #316 MERGED feat/twins-die-with-boss: 2026-10-20 ADDS-DIE-WITH-BOSS (feat/twins-die-with-boss): #297 had already made the Twins die with the boss; the remaining gap was Echo adds (standing, or due on the death step) surviving the boss to run_end, fixed in EchoSpawns.Step with red-then-green AddsDieWithBossTests, no golden moved (Matt 2026-10-20 decision, f-77fcfca4).
- 2026-10-20 #318 MERGED feat/private-rating: PRIVATE-RATING adds a private WoW-style M+ rating (sum of each dungeon's best timed run, integer key-and-time score) kept in the local profile and drawn only on the player's own Screen, with tests proving it never reaches the queue payload, the run trace or a log line.
- 2026-10-20 #320 MERGED docs/agent-playtesters: AGENT-PLAYTESTERS: design/PLAYTESTERS.md and STATE.md D33 define agent playtesters that start from a player-side guide, fail their first key, learn through receipted runs and memory, and prove the learning against an amnesiac control.
- 2026-10-20 #323 MERGED fix/telegraph-cancel: 2026-10-20 TELEGRAPH-CANCEL (fix/telegraph-cancel, based on fix/slam-telegraphs #313): red TelegraphCancelTests/TelegraphEndTests first, then CancelUnlanded in the run root and the view's no-flash free; no oracle fixture moved.
- 2026-10-20 #328 MERGED fix/seed-teaches-core-rule: 2026-10-20 SEED-BLOOM-TEACHES (fix/seed-teaches-core-rule): verified real (the seedbloom Window expired with no Combo; bots fled the enemy-opened patch as hostile and then followed the leader out); fixed in Keyrun.Bots with a layout-gated encounter-Window hold, red-then-green SeedBloomTeachesTests in CI.
- 2026-10-20 #332 MERGED fix/gear-into-run: 2026-10-20 GEAR-INTO-RUN (fix/gear-into-run): SimDriver.StartRun now creates each run from RunStart.Of, which stacks the screen's equipped gear (keystone included) on the queued Clause and talents, so equipped Facets change the run; RunStartGearTests red on base by compile (CS0246 RunStart).
- 2026-10-20 #337 MERGED fix/cinder-overheat-host: CINDER-OVERHEAT-HOST wires the content's Heat/Overheat rule into the host run (HostRunSetup.OverheatRulesOf); the Cinder seat now opens at 0 Heat and overheats at 100 in the real game. Not engine-verified by the lane (no launches): the e2e Cinder-damage-before-pull-1 assertion should be watched on game-e2e.
- 2026-10-20 #338 MERGED fix/cinderfield-closer-cost: CINDERFIELD-CLOSER-COST (f-17c18460): the Cinderfield closer is still burned, now 15% of max health through damage reduction and never more than 20%, so no key, Clause or gear power can make closing it a one-shot (Matt 2026-10-20, D34).
- 2026-10-20 #339 MERGED fix/cinder-chill-clears-self: CINDER-CHILL-SELF Cold Snap and Flashfire now clear the Cinder's own Chill instead of the target's (C#-only, gated on the Overheat rule; oracle unchanged). Live in the game once the host sets RunContent.Overheat (PR #337, f-180719b5).
- 2026-10-20 #340 MERGED fix/kf1-gait-cadence: 2026-10-20 KF1-GAIT (fix/kf1-gait-cadence): every bound body's run or walk loop played at 1x, so feet skated 1.3x to 2.6x their stride; GaitRule plays the loop at sim speed over the clip's planted-foot speed at the model's scale, red-then-green on CI; frames still need the orchestrator's eye.
- 2026-10-20 #341 MERGED fix/untimed-key-no-deplete: 2026-10-20 UNTIMED-KEEPS-KEY (fix/untimed-key-no-deplete): the end-of-run key rule moves into the tested sim call Timer.KeyAfterRun, so an untimed key keeps its level while the +1/+2/+3 timed jumps stay unchanged. KeyAfterRunTests went red in CI against a depleting mutant (key - 1) and green with the real rule; main already kept the key, so this is a lock-in, not a behaviour change.
- 2026-10-20 #342 MERGED docs/record-matt-decisions-1020: RECORD-MATT-DECISIONS moved eight Matt 2026-10-20 decisions from the aesop open-findings ledger into STATE.md D35-D42 with their BIBLE sections and build status (D39 and D42 built in #304; D37 and D41 partly; D35, D36 and D40 open in PRs #308 and #318).
- 2026-10-20 #343 MERGED fix/run-end-key-kept-copy: 2026-10-20 KEY-KEPT-COPY (fix/run-end-key-kept-copy): the run-end screen stops saying "key depleted" for an untimed run, which read as a key drop, and names the level the key keeps ("key stays at +N"); tests pushed red first, then the text change.
- 2026-10-20 #345 MERGED feat/stacked-nameplates-castbars: STACKED-PLATES moves enemy cast bars onto their name plates, stacks the plates vertically without overlap in a stable order, marks kickable casts with an amber diamond (locked: grey square), and makes a hovered enemy plate the mouseover unit for the Q kick (f-e933a2b7).
- 2026-10-20 #346 MERGED fix/enemy-engage: 2026-10-20 ENEMY-ENGAGE (fix/enemy-engage): melee enemies in reach of their target now turn to face it before they swing, in both the C# sim and the frozen TS twin, with the bot oracle traces, the content-pull golden and their recorded hashes and encounter tallies re-recorded (TS twin red with the turn removed: facing error 512/1024 in range, 99/1024 on a sidestep).
- 2026-10-20 #347 MERGED fix/movie-frames-from-trace: MOVIE-FRAMES-FROM-TRACE made --write-movie capture the chase, the must-kick cast bar and the after-kick frame on the ticks their trace events pick, and log the event and tick each frame was taken on, so the proof frames show what they claim when pull timing drifts.
- 2026-10-20 #348 MERGED fix/reward-quality-from-key-level: REWARD-KEY-QUALITY made the reward tier band a function of key level only per f-baeb031a, so a depleted or abandoned key-15+ run can roll tiers 9-10, with the abandoned key-20 golden row re-derived as a documented break from the frozen TS.
- 2026-10-20 #349 MERGED feat/hud-forces-bar: HUD-FORCES adds the Mythic+ Enemy Forces bar under the key timer, read from the run state and forces.json.
- 2026-10-20 #350 MERGED feat/queue-role-scarcity-ready-check: QUEUE-ROLES-READY adds the BIBLE s10 role-scarcity readout and the 10-second ready check on pop (pure model in Keyrun.Sim, wired into the Queue HUD node).
- 2026-10-20 #351 MERGED fix/telegraph-honest: TELEGRAPH-HONEST withholds a spansCast telegraph whose cast a later same-tick step takes over (CastHeldAt); red TelegraphHonestTests first, no oracle fixture moved.
- 2026-10-20 #352 MERGED fix/kf1-spawn-hold: 2026-10-20 KF1-SPAWN-HOLD (fix/kf1-spawn-hold): the last unowned KF1 part, the hold state, fixed in the view layer only so it serves spawn-on-entry and prespawn alike; red commit carries a stub with main's behaviour.
- 2026-10-20 #354 MERGED test/combo-tables: COMBO-TABLES-TEST: ContentCombos.ToComboTables (moved from HostRunSetup into Keyrun.Bots.Shared) is pinned by ContentCombosTests against the real combos.json and combo-riders.json: every declared cell present, none invented, "*" rows fanned out, riders applied.
- 2026-10-20 #355 MERGED fix/cast-label-name: CAST-LABEL-NAME stops the over-head cast bar repeating the caster's name under its own name plate, so the bar's width goes to the ability the player must read; a plateless caster keeps "Name: Ability" with the name cut first.
- 2026-10-20 #358 MERGED fix/fork-sidegrade: FORK-SIDEGRADE makes the fork parity check fail on identical sibling options and on duplicate runs, replaces the dead-seed key-2 run with a dial-90 seed-11 run whose seed is proven live, and retunes Chorus Discipline (Mend heal 120 -> 200) so Round Call, Discipline and Encore each win a named run; oracle frozenSourceHash 2c243dd9 -> 73fde0bb and content-pull contentHash 6b38cef7 -> 7351a162 re-recorded with the repo tools for the content change (they also cover #356's content change).
- 2026-10-20 #361 MERGED fix/runend-card-tier-bonus: RUNEND-CARD-TIER-BONUS: two cards for one Facet at Tier 1 and Tier 2 now read differently, each naming its tier's stat bonus from gear.json.
- 2026-10-20 #368 MERGED fix/facets-applied-once: FACETS-APPLIED-ONCE stopped the between-runs Queue from handing the run a Facet-patched kit that the host then patched again, so every gear Facet now applies once.
- 2026-10-20 #371 MERGED fix/combat-anim-readability: 2026-10-20 KF1-WINDUP (fix/combat-anim-readability): enemy melee casts looped Spellcasting and snapped to the blow; now each strike winds up over its bar and lands on the resolve frame, and nine abilities get their own clip; AbilityAnimTests red on a stub of the old behaviour (20 of 25) and on two production mutants (6 of 28), green on the fix.
- 2026-10-20 #374 MERGED fix/seed-cheese: 2026-10-20 SEED-LOCK-TEACHES (fix/seed-cheese): the Seed now opens a 3 s Lock-style Window on itself 20 ticks into its pull, so the party must land a Combo on it while it lives instead of bursting it and meeting the rule only in the free post-fight bloom (KF3, f-70c82aca).
- 2026-10-20 #381 MERGED feat/skippable-packs: SKIPPABLE-PACKS ships two optional forces packs beside the Hollow Vault walking line so the route to 100% forces is a choice.
- 2026-10-20 #385 MERGED fix/untimed-key-hud-label: 2026-10-20 UNTIMED-KEY-GUARD (fix/untimed-key-hud-label): the HUD run clock bar said DEPLETED after a lasting wipe, which a WoW player reads as the key dropping; it now says "Wiped -- key stays +N", and a band-wide guard proves no untimed or abandoned run lowers the key. Red first (3 label cases), green after; a key-1 depleting mutant in KeyAfterRun/KeyMove reddens 2 guard facts.
- 2026-10-20 #386 MERGED test/reward-quality-key-guard: REWARD-KEY-GUARD added five behavioural guards for f-baeb031a (loot tier from key level, chest only moves the key, tier cap 10), each proven red by a production mutant and green on main.
- 2026-10-20 #389 MERGED feat/telegraph-every-enemy-ability: TELEGRAPH-EVERY-ABILITY adds a content-and-play guard that every damaging or controlling enemy cast shows a bar and a shaped floor telegraph from cast start, at least 10 ticks before it lands, at keys 2, 10 and 20.
- 2026-10-20 #395 MERGED feat/lobby-tabs: LOBBY-TABS turns the between-runs screen into one Character / Build / Play lobby (D50): the class picked on Character, its talents and the equipped gear's tiers reach the queued run, and the run end's choice of three returns to Play; 10 LobbyTabsTests added (Keyrun.Playtest.Tests 71 to 81).
- 2026-10-21 #244 CLOSED fix/sim-cloud-ids-unique: CLOUD-ID made combo cloud ids unique when one actor opens two clouds in the same tick, so each cloud keeps its own id and telegraph instead of two sharing one, with single-cloud ids unchanged.
- 2026-10-21 #280 MERGED test/text-matches-data-coverage: TEXT-RULES adds rule tests for the five uncovered text families and a clauses.json load/shape/text suite, and pins three tooltip-vs-sim mismatches as known so the text fix lane must strike each one when it lands.
- 2026-10-21 #293 MERGED fix/ability-out-of-range-readout: RANGE-READOUT: an out-of-range ability slot now dims and draws a distance-arrow glyph beside its red tint, and a press that fails for range shows a 1.5 s "Out of range" line; RangeReadoutTests pins it.
- 2026-10-21 #307 CLOSED feat/holder-lock: HOLDER-LOCK (D30): engaged enemies lock onto the nearest player at engage and only a taunt or the holder's death moves them; damage, healing and threat no longer pull aggro; oracle and goldens re-recorded.
- 2026-10-21 #308 CLOSED feat/prespawn-aggro: PRESPAWN-AGGRO makes every Hollow Vault group stand idle in its room from tick 0 and engage on its 4000 mm aggro ring (or on damage, or by link) instead of spawning on room entry, and teaches the bots and the scripted Warden to hold outside the ring until the party is in, then pull with the tank first; PATH-TO-BOSS (D44) then removes every gate, so the party walks one open path to the boss past prespawned packs, a run wipes once no living seated player remains, and the bots follow the lowest living seat; the Seed elite no longer spawns on entry (KF3). CHOIR-PACK-HEAL then makes Choral Mending heal the whole Choir pack for half its health, so an unkicked Cantor drags the Choir past the clock and a party that kicks it still times the key.
- 2026-10-21 #311 MERGED docs/skill-toolkit-direction: SKILL-TOOLKIT recorded D31 (Outlaw-level toolkit inside the five-button budget, via Facets and forks and generic take/give/drop threat ops) with a sourced Outlaw research brief (research/12-skill-toolkit.md); nothing implemented; BIBLE conflicts listed in the PR for Matt.
- 2026-10-21 #319 MERGED feat/bots-gear: Bot seats now wear a default five-slot gear loadout resolved through the same GearFacets.Resolve path as the human, carried into every host run as RunOptions.Loadouts.

- 2026-10-21 #330 MERGED fix/seedbloom-no-stakes: SEEDBLOOM-NO-STAKES (f-ff4ce040): an empty combo rider for seedbloom-field means closing the Seed's Death Bloom no longer hits the closer for 120, while the Combo still earns the Seed bonus.
- 2026-10-21 #333 CLOSED fix/pair-marks-consequences: PAIR-MARKS gives the Pair's spread and stack marks real damage (spread 80 to allies within 3000 mm, stack 150 split by headcount within 1500 mm) through a C#-only MarkRules built from run.json pairMarks; oracle runs unchanged.
- 2026-10-21 #335 CLOSED fix/lock-telegraph: 2026-10-20 LOCK-TELEGRAPH (fix/lock-telegraph): red first (TS lock-rule and C# Lock tests), then the Lock cast bar and circle; oracle re-recorded (golden content-pull contentHash, k2/k10/content-pull setups, bots-hv-k10-s7 traceHash c076efdb -> 96b75ecc, frozenSourceHash d1bd1349), C# trace pins re-measured (k10 telegraphs and hits 16 -> 17, all-trace telegraphs 46 -> 47).
- 2026-10-21 #344 MERGED fix/stun-stops-enemies: STUN-STOPS-ENEMIES makes the Break-bar stun stop the Twins (f-8b3fff18): a staggered Twin's pending slam ends with cast_stopped and it starts no cast, swing or move until the stun expires. Before, the boss golden showed slams landing at ticks 70 and 130, inside the 45-145 stun.

- 2026-10-21 #366 MERGED fix/facets-change-function: FACETS-CHANGE-FUNCTION made the five Chanter verse Facets and the two line-width Facets change what their abilities do in the sim, with Overture of Power redefined as an off-GCD Refrain because a Verse refund cannot coexist with a binary verse swap.
- 2026-10-21 #388 CLOSED feat/path-to-boss-packs: PATH-PACKS puts a required trash pack on the Hollow Vault walking line and makes every layout pack pull as one group, so the run walks pull, pack, pull, boss and the forces bar fills only with enough packs.
- 2026-10-21 #415 MERGED fix/bots-cadence-setup: CADENCE-SETUP: the Chanter bot opens Attuned on a damage partner's enemy during heal downtime, so Attuned combos close in bot play (0 -> 25 over the seeded runs); oracle re-recorded. Follow-up: five dominated talent options buffed (fork parity green), the encounter trace model tracks in-flight casts, the hud test skip reverted; run F's abandon is pinned to the first pull (`2@pull0+1350`, R-415-D), not a run tick. Run C on the host clears again: the healer's Setup reads its gear-patched shape, so a party-wide Cadence is never aimed at an enemy.
